# Redactions and derivation log

Original files remain unchanged. FILE-MANIFEST.json records result origins and
original/current SHA-256; SOURCE-MANIFEST.json records source provenance. Derived
topology records list input hashes; a derivative's hash is not the hash of a raw input.

## Rules

- Operator home paths become /operator-home; a named private operator becomes OPERATOR. These replacements affect metadata/docs only, never measurement values.
- uname hostnames become HOST; UID/GID ownership and group membership strings are removed. Kernel/version/CPU/filesystem data and timestamps remain.
- Cgroup membership paths receive stable opaque scope names, preserving equality for identical original paths. Controller and mount information remains. Numeric PIDs/TIDs are retained as ephemeral within-run identities.
- UUIDs/resource identifiers, if matched in selected material, receive named placeholders. No generic numerical redaction is used: CPU counters, timings, worker maxima, hashes and revisions are not identifiers to erase.
- Global before.txt/after.txt process snapshots are excluded entirely, not selectively published. Scoped task metadata is restricted to the benchmark Tgid and iobaseline/iou-wrk comm names; scoped observation samples contain no global ps command arguments.
- Four local-path/history-dependent legacy shell runners are omitted rather than silently edited. Go source remains byte-identical. Script relocation, the helper-path update and the pre-existing public helper difference are recorded in [source provenance](../docs/provenance.md).

## Final pre-upstream controls

The new group adds selected files from private `pre-upstream-controls/` at
execution revision `ec82557a9e295fcff895234212e76ad01b8cec19`. FILE-MANIFEST.json
records each original and public digest, selection scope and transformation.
Commands replace operator home paths with `${REPO}` / `${GO_ROOT}` and recorded
operator UID/GID with named variables. Reviewed environment metadata omits the
hostname, unrelated disks, cgroup session and private artifact inventory, while
preserving kernel, CPU, RAM, filesystem/mount options, Go revision and dataset hash.

The operator explicitly authorized changing only `dataset.path` in each of the
24 new canonical JSON files to `data/readat-512MiB.bin`. These files are metadata-
sanitized derivatives, **not byte-identical originals**. Every other byte is
verified unchanged, and all original/public SHA-256 pairs are in FILE-MANIFEST.json.
The 60 existing Phase 7/8 canonical JSON and all private originals remain intact.

Perf excerpts select only the first iobaseline symbol block from each existing
`report-symbolized.txt`; unrelated process blocks are omitted. Kernel addresses
and original percentages remain unchanged, but the denominator is system-wide.
These excerpts are neither complete callchains nor an exclusive CPU breakdown.
All raw perf.data and full system-wide reports remain private. The digest of a
privileged raw perf input is left unknown where unavailable rather than invented;
the public build-ID extraction has its own checksum. The vmlinux report command
was operator-reported, not preserved in an original command log.

The final public summary is an explicitly updated derivative of the private
summary, whose pending-perf wording predates the operator recordings. No private
summary or historical artifact was changed. Existing Phase 7/8 canonical JSON
remain unchanged; the new run-data privacy/identity check is reported separately.

## Derived topology

topology-derived.json recomputes submit-owner set comparison from result.json and selected task metadata. It retains sample count, TIDs/owners, observed distinct workers, P value and revision. Original summary.txt remains available even when its parser reported empty sets. Historical Phase 6C wait-TID values are explicitly invalid; they are not repaired or used for submit-owner matching. Default C sampling is insufficient for quantitative heavy-observer claims.

## Omitted data

No .git, generated ELF, source dataset, cloud session record, full tarball, private key, global process snapshot or build cache is exported. Omitted private originals are not claimed to be sanitized. Dataset digest and generation instructions replace dataset distribution.
